Its kernel is exactly the two-step global-unit norm image, with psi_n(u^3)=theta_n(u), both for actual units and their pro-three completions. The fixed U-cube obstruction consequently persists over every shifted base of the same tower. A.172 classifies every locally matching full metabelian tripod fibre over a cuspidal full abelian pair. Each fibre consists of its one or two explicitly listed actual cusp sections. A.175 proves the actual unit field-norm index3^5 and S-unit field-norm index3^10 for E3/E2. A.172 classifies every locally matching full metabelian tripod fibre over a cuspidal full abelian pair over every number field. A.177 proves at every finite height that the whole three-primary class group of E_n is generated by its six primes above11 and59, that both S-three-class groups vanish, and that the next actual S-unit field-norm index is3^(2n+6). A.179 proves that every pure-field three-class group in the fixed tower is cyclic and generated by R59,n. Hence the entire balanced quotient C_n^bal is zero at every finite level. A.178 classifies every locally point/cusp-matching section with density-one finite proper support in the actual quotient E_X/H_3′ and in the full geometric metabelian quotient, for every hyperbolic curve over a number field. Each is the image of a unique actual full point or cusp class. It also determines every rational Abel–Jacobi fibre, including its complete injective cusp parameter. A.180 identifies the exact image of the actual infinite norm fibre: one stable integer valuation profile must satisfy the full ordinary principal-ideal tests and its actual norm-one-unit defect must vanish. It determines all completed valuation profiles by a single equation in the inverse cyclic three-class group, and constructs the exact connecting map from the completed-unit lattice quotients. A.181 evaluates a nonzero actual and completed derived-limit norm class in the fixed tower over Q(cuberoot(3)). The explicit unit B=(alpha^2-2)^3 is an actual field norm from every finite height, but has no infinite compatible actual or completed family. A.183 evaluates the first-weight image of the A181 obstruction as the zero cup kappa(A) cup kappa(U^3), with its positive sign fixed. Its least detecting quotient index lies between two and fourteen, and its nonzero image belongs to the specified coefficient-power lim1 subgroup. A.184 determines every one-step global-unit norm image as the kernel of its two tame characters, proves those characters vanish on all relative norm-one global units, and constructs the resulting secondary homomorphism on that actual norm image. Its kernel is exactly the two-step global-unit norm image, with psi_n(u^3)=theta_n(u), both for actual units and their pro-three completions. The fixed U-cube obstruction consequently persists over every shifted base of the same tower. The unit field-norm index is27 and the root-invariant three-class subgroup has order27. The actual ideal norm M→E2 induces isomorphisms from both ordinary and S-localized root coinvariants onto Cl(E2){3}=C3³; ideal extension into the S-localized group is zero. A.194 constructs the subgroup B generated by the four actual cubic intermediate-field unit groups in M/F. The literal group-ring norm-sum identity gives3U_M contained in B. A.195 proves that the explicit A.181 norm obstruction has least nonzero integral weight exactly2. Over G_(k,S), its weight-two image is zero modulo3 and nonzero modulo9. The actual integral inflation is injective and gives a nonzero absolute continuous weight-two class, while every absolute finite-coefficient image remains zero. The class is therefore explicitly in the coefficient-power derived-limit subgroup. A.196 constructs the literal class-three arithmetic quotient Q=((Z3[T]/T³)y) semidirect Z3x of the tripod over k=Q(cube-root3). The prescribed frame A=649^8 alpha, B=(alpha²−2)³ has a continuous global class-two lift and continuous Q-lifts at every place, but no continuous global Q-lift. Every finite quotient Q_c of geometric order3^(4c) has a global lift; the continuous obstruction has exact order3. The ninth-power cover and all three actual tangent maps show that the local frame at3 has no ordinary point or cusp image. A.197 supplies an actual global unit r in the degree54 ninth-root field, with a compact factorization r=c r0 and the exact identity r³ e3 e6 e9=e1 e5 e8 u2 u4. A.201 proves that every full profinite class-two section of the tripod over Q whose finite local restrictions match actual point or cusp sections has a continuous global full profinite class-three lift. Both weight-three continuous obstruction classes vanish, including their derived-limit components, with no finite-support condition on the Kummer valuations. A.202 refines the actual ordinary-ghost recursion for every prescribed finite geometric quotient Q. It supplies all-locally ordinary infinite-support sections of E_(Q,A) with point-three modulo-eight frame and every sufficiently large exact dyadic separation depth E. Their abelian pair has a global full class-two lift, while a specified later quotient of geometric order2^(4E−5) and class4E−8 excludes every everywhere locally matching full-metabelian lift. Every finite-place restriction is injective. The three coefficient projections at(3,2),(2,3),(2,5) give an exact CRT normalization; all remaining local conditions retain their stated tests. An ordinary finite local image leaves at most one globally matching candidate, and failure is witnessed at at most two places. The actual ideal norm onto Cl(E2){3}=C3^3 has order-three kernel. Every three-primary E2 ideal class capitulates in M. Every one of the eight local sheets of the cyclic-eight genus-two cover fails the complete localized two-Selmer image. The complete next tame unit-norm matrix has rank3, giving unit field-norm index27 and root-invariant three-class order3^7 in the next field. Every one of its24 continuous global class-two lifts has the point-three image in the actual order-eight dihedral quotient. The explicit rational cover has eight local point-three sheets and central-involution Frobenius over the prescribed ordinary residue828. The local correction group is C16 and the global correction sends k inC24 to6k. The discrepancies are exactly all eight odd classes, each occurring three times. A.211 proves the exact cyclotomic norm identity for the alignment scalar, normalizes the Hilbert90 factor into the base field while preserving the original unit preimage, and constructs the actual lower capitulating ideal class. Its two changed-prime coordinates give the secondary character by multiplication by2. A.182 constructs a rational isogeny from the genus-twenty-one pointed Fermat-eight Jacobian to three elliptic curves and nine abelian surfaces. Every factor is rationally simple and has an explicit infinite-order rational point, giving rank at least twelve. Every nonzero rational abelian quotient has positive rank; the actual Weil-restriction adjunction then excludes every nonconstant map over any number field to an abelian variety with a finite group of points over that field. A.185 constructs three explicit rational isogenies from the Prym factors to Weil restrictions of elliptic curves over Q(sqrt(-3)), Q(sqrt(2)) and Q(sqrt(-6)). Exact two-isogeny connecting images give their ranks 1, 2 and 2. The three rational elliptic factors each have rank 1. The actual Jacobian decomposition therefore gives rank J(C8)(Q)=8 plus the sum of the six genus-two ranks, and hence rank at least 14. A.186 proves rank one, rational torsion Z/2, a two-dimensional two-Selmer group, and zero two-primary Tate–Shafarevich group for each of the six genus-two factors in A.182. The six literal quartic-field S-unit bases and seventeen local point tests give exhaustive upper bounds, matching the rational divisor classes already proved in A.182. Together with A.185 and the actual factor isogeny, this gives rank J(C8)(Q)=14 and genus 21. The actual A.182 isogeny then gives a finite two-primary Tate–Shafarevich group for J(C8), zero T2Sha, and the full continuous two-adic Selmer image equal to the completed rank-fourteen Mordell–Weil group. The proof gives all twelve literal generic fibers and exact curve degrees, then computes the actual trace/pullback operators on all twenty-one character lines. Consequently the kernel of F is killed by16, its degree is a power of two, 32 annihilates Sha(J(C8))[2-infinity], and the image from Selmer level 2^(n+5) to level 2^n is exactly the Mordell–Weil Kummer image. Its explicit Abel–Jacobi logarithm computation compares the global slope1 modulo4 with slope3 modulo4 for every other point in that disc. Through the actual Fermat map this proves C8(Q)={(±1,±1)}. Every locally point-or-cusp-matching section of the full metabelian tripod extension in finite Kummer class([3],[-2]) consequently has ordinary dyadic centre3, with global first-factor compact two-adic class D or−D. A.190 proves injectivity of completed Mordell–Weil localization at2 for all twelve factors and for J(C8). The two rank-two quadratic elliptic factors are treated by an exact conjugate-isogeny determinant and a rational formal-logarithm certificate with rigorous infinite-tail bounds. The actual Prym and Jacobian reverse maps transfer injectivity with the rational torsion comparison. Combining A.187–A.189, the full continuous two-adic Selmer intersection with C8(Q2), including its global class, is exactly the four rational point pairs (kappa2(j(P)),P). A.192 determines the entire continuous two-adic Selmer intersection on C:y²=x⁵−54x, including its global coordinate: the four pairs at infinity, (0,0), and (3,±9), with classes0,T,D,−D. The actual cyclic degree-eight cover z⁸=−t(1−t)³/24 has compactification C. In its single finite Kummer class[-24], every locally matching full metabelian section has dyadic ordinary centre3 or cusp0 or1, with the corresponding exact compact coefficient class. A.193 gives the actual local completion J(Q2)^completion=Z2D plus Z2E* plus C2T and the exact finite Selmer membership test on every dyadic disc of y²=x⁵−54x. The cyclic-eight cover supplies the arithmetic quotient with geometric group (Z2[zeta8]/2^n) semidirect C8, of order2^(4n+3) and nilpotency class4n. ## Work record

Work began at approximately 00:47 UTC on 16 November 2026. This is an active incomplete manuscript. The complete prompt and attached archive inventory were read. The supplied sources are omitted from checkpoints. Three parallel mathematical audits accompany the root work on the completed unit equation and the literal assertion (G). The main classification and lifting problems remain on the page.

## Current advances and remaining steps

The main finite-ramification route proves residual irreducibility and an equivalence between uniform residual support and finite ramification. Neither argument supplies the missing uniform bound. Single-coefficient locally elliptic infinitely ramified representations have been constructed with their exact forgetful comparison; no all-coefficient section is asserted to realize them.

For the actual ordinary completed unit equation, A.27 computes the full continuous cup as zero at every coefficient prime, including its first derived inverse-limit component. The strict tame-residue map sends the original cup to alpha*b-beta*a-(q-1)*a*b/2. The ordinary local valuation patterns give a primitive in the required cofinite-convergence module; the 2-primary term uses the integral binomial correction. A.38 identifies this with the actual full profinite class-two lifting obstruction.

A.44 settles the existence part of L9. Its products A=3 product(q_j^((j+4)!)) and B=-2 product(r_j^((j+4)!)) have disjoint infinite new-prime supports and ordinary nonzero local values summing to one at every finite prime. The exact radical-field commutator calculation identifies the maximal abelian subfield and makes each Chebotarev congruence request compatible. A self-contained Jacobi-sum estimate handles all remaining large primes, and explicit Hensel precision inequalities handle the coefficient primes. Three agents independently checked the proof. The finite certificates were actually run; they do not claim execution of the enormous first-stage prime search.

A.40 proves that the class-two fibre has exactly 24 classes, with an explicit localization map. A.43 computes the actual weight-three cocycles and their local residues. At the permanent valuations at 2 and 3 of A.44, these residues force every possible class-three lift to project to one distinguished class-two class. For the original A.44 pair, that specific full obstruction remains to be evaluated. A.58–A.59 construct a further pair whose full obstruction vanishes. Local class-two point matching remains an additional exact test.

A.46 identifies the entire continuous weight-three group with the literal residue quotient R_ell/(q²−1)R_ell. Its two explicit families retain the possible derived-limit contribution. A.52 proves that the original ghost's distinguished lift survives the third layer modulo24 while retaining the full integral class-two quotient. Starting the recursion at K! gives survival moduloK!, so every prescribed finite third layer admits such an infinite-support example. These are different choices of ghost as the modulus grows. The proof explicitly handles the two-primary binomial discrepancy and kills its two finite square-class cups by Brauer reciprocity; it does not infer integral vanishing from the finite assertions.

A.47–A.48 construct ordinary infinite-support data for every fixed finite set of rational punctures. A.50 lifts every ordinary such datum to the full profinite class-two quotient: the pair-forgetful affine maps detect every component of the exterior-square obstruction. A.51 separately identifies the exact fibre product retaining one finite geometric quotient and the full abelianization, and constructs ghost sections there. In A.53 the actual Legendre projective level-four group is V4, with its displayed quotient map from S4; the odd-level compactifications are X(6), X(10) and X(14), not the curves over the j-line before base change. The full linear-to-projective maps and arithmetic extension maps are written out.

After finite ramification, the integral labeled lattice gives an actual Legendre curve. Its geometric conjugator is controlled by an exact determinant-square class. Ordinary multiplicative places give a further comparison, and the mod-5 invariant-line criterion over Q(i) recognizes the j=1728 case through X0(20). At most one local cusp label also gives a point. The exact GL2(F3)-to-S3 resolvent is v^3=4t(1-t). It recognizes the first mixed-label cases, while the saturated labeled T2 line identifies all odd-prime degeneration colours. A prime-supported coordinate and the opposite cusp logarithm give a whole arithmetic recognition family. Together with the finite 2-adic contact and cubic-orientation calculations, every integer realization from 2 through 31 is recognized. A.54 retains the constant256 in the formal Tate parameter q=C²/256 times a unit and uses determinant one of the H-conjugator. An opposite odd cusp forces the coefficient-p exponent of2 to vanish, giving Tate valuation ratio -4/(m-4), which must be a square unit in Z_p. For parameter33 it is -4, excluded at both3 and11. A primitive-isogeny argument then identifies the exact rational parameter. The unrestricted full congruence-kernel cocycle problem remains.

Central puncture lifts have an explicit compatible splitting. A chosen central lift can fail to admit a birational extension; this does not refute the existence of another lift. The configuration-space interpretation and two-variable diagonal inertia identify the exact missing full lifting maps. An actual rank-two valuation gives a full lift in one genuinely bad marked-reduction case.

The cyclotomic countersection is a full continuous section, obtained from a degree-nine cover with no rational compactification point and projectivity of the absolute Galois group. The degree-27 Heisenberg cover distinguishes the two sixth-root points first in lower-central class two. These are assertions over the specified cyclotomic field, with explicit comparison maps; they do not assert a counterexample over Q.

For D1, finite individual localization images and injectivity into their product have not yielded a finite global image. The positive-density promotion has concrete trace and Frobenius-polynomial countermodels, but no curve with infinitely many sections is constructed. For D2 and D3 the remaining step is generic reconstruction; the test tower has no generic section. For D5 the functor is faithful and detects dominance of actual maps; arbitrary open outer homomorphisms have not been reconstructed as ring maps.

A.55 determines the image of global central corrections allowed to ramify only at a fixed set and at completely split auxiliary primes. It is the annihilator of the explicit finite dual cohomology group. A cyclotomic–Kummer cocycle of exact order16 supplies an explicit failed correction at73 for c=2·73^24. The same statement proves that a finite correction preserves an integral class-two lift exactly when its coefficient Bockstein vanishes, equivalently unramifiedness outside the coefficient prime and the required real vanishing at2. These tests have not yet been evaluated on the actual distinguished ghost discrepancy.

A.56 computes the entire geometric Legendre abelian quotient as (Z/8)^2 plus Z/3, with the exact Kummer map (a,b) to (a mod8,b mod8,a+b mod3). Compact-image arguments prove the full arithmetic fibre product with abelianization. The constructed ghosts can therefore be combined with the entire congruence representation of the rational point3 in that quotient. Their fresh-prime local points have nonzero Tate inertia while that rational-point representation has good reduction, so these quotient sections fail the actual local comparison. The cyclic Kummer cover z^n=t^a(1-t)^b factors through this quotient precisely when n divides24a,24b,8(a-b); the full two-coordinate Fermat cover does so precisely when n divides8.

A.57 computes the abelian characters contained in every field factor of the fibre at a new rational puncture by an explicit finite stabilizer problem. Its common abelian field lies in the compositum of the relevant quadratic radical fields and the two-power cyclotomic field. Thus only two-primary characters remain in this initialization problem. Explicit cyclotomic norm identities prove the required constancy in an opposite-radicand family. A separate search of281,664 two-adic candidates in a product-relation family found32,028 actual locally soluble choices and no varying Brauer evaluation; this finite search is recorded, with no general conclusion inferred.

A.58–A.59 settle the universal weight-three obstruction hunch by an actual counterexample. The three-prime recursion has homogeneous coefficients a=3u^24, b=-2v^24, c=w^24 and fresh exponents24 n_j(p²−1). It maintains unit D_j-points at every finite prime, where D_j=48n_j², and increasing precision for a+b-c. Exact local power images handle coefficient primes, a triangular CRT preserves every smaller-prime dependency, and a maximal-abelian field calculation makes the three Chebotarev requests compatible. The ordinary limits have positive numerator support and common negative denominator support. Their divided weight-three residues lie in n_j Z_ell and therefore in the required c0 module. This gives one continuous section of the actual full class-three quotient. Its full local class-three point compatibility and its lifting to the metabelian or full fundamental group are not asserted. Root and all three agents independently audited the construction.

A.60–A.62 extend the actual lift through the full fourth central layer. A single ordinary infinite-support full class-four lift is therefore constructed. Full local class-four point matching at every prime is not claimed.

A.63 uses the coefficient-prime Tate representation and the proved p-adic Mahler–Manin theorem. A rational elliptic curve’s nonzero Tate Kummer class cannot lie on a line generated by one rational number: valuation would force a rational proportionality scalar and then an algebraic Tate parameter, contradicting the theorem. The exact formal cusp classes are A²/256, B²/256, and A⁻²/256. A coordinate supported only at2 excludes two labels and A.37 recognizes the section. This yields the full dyadic parameter family under arbitrary integral labeled realization, with no determinant or parity restriction. The remaining larger-support problem is recorded through explicit coefficient-prime logarithm equations and a common global Weil multiplier.

A.64 applies the exact mod-eight quotient of the geometric congruence group to lambda=±16p^n. Potential good reduction at2 and the two mod-eight valuation classes leave exactly the ordinary valuation pair(4,0). The completed coordinate is therefore ±16p^beta. Its possible cusp atp would have formal Tate class p^(2beta), which A.63 excludes. The ordinary valuation atp makes beta an integer, giving a rational Kummer coordinate and hence the point section. The proof retains the H-conjugator hypothesis and verifies its transfer under each of the six cusp permutations.

A.65–A.66 prove the fixed-depth conclusion in A5: for every R and N there is an ordinary infinite-support ghost with an actual full integral class-R section agreeing with the rational point3 in the finite truncated Magnus quotient moduloN. This does not set an unknown K-group to zero. The pair may depend on R,N, and no compatible family as R varies is inferred.
